Use when device data calls repeat per iteration and slow reviews. Not for cases where the customer genuinely changes device mid session, which should trigger a fresh fetch by design. Use when closed cases resurrect without new activity. Not for rings with genuinely new linked transactions, which should reopen with the new evidence cited. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fraud-agent-re-opened-resolved-fraud-r-46a88da516e487ea))
- **fraud-agent-re-screened-cleared-transa-8ec458fa3e4a0b1e**: Stops fraud agents re-screening already cleared transactions after cache invalidation: persists screening outcomes durably keyed by transaction and list version, and treats cache loss as a performance event, not a correctness reset. Use when a cache flush triggers a full re-screen storm. Not for deliberate rescreening after a sanctions list update, which is required and should be planned. - **fraud-agent-s-evidence-pack-download-t-099c4b82702a97a9**: Fixes fraud agent evidence downloads that time out on large PDF batches: downloads per document with streaming and resumable ranges, enforces per file timeouts, and processes files as they land instead of after the whole batch. Use when evidence packs include scanned bundles over tens of megabytes. Not for small evidence files failing instantly, which points at auth or URL errors instead. - **fraud-agent-s-ofac-batch-screen-timed-9c8c2680157e5650**: Keeps fraud agent OFAC batch screening alive through sanctions API latency spikes: chunks batches, sets per call timeouts with partial result handling, and retries only the unscreened remainder. Use when batch screens die on a single slow call. Not for screening logic errors, where calls return fast with wrong results. - **fraud-agent-s-pdf-parser-dropped-charg-9a4c33e8cde54e03**: Fixes agent PDF parsers that silently drop chargeback evidence pages: counts pages on ingest, extracts per page with failure tracking, and blocks filing when extracted pages do not match the source. - **fraud-agent-s-rule-test-ran-against-pr-0fde464163c4509f**: Isolates fraud agent rule testing from production: runs tests against a mirrored sandbox or historical replay, blocks test execution when the target environment is live, and tags test traffic so it can never train models or trigger fulfilment. Use when an agent test evaluated rules on real orders. Not for shadow mode on live traffic, which observes real orders without acting and is a separate approved pattern. - **fraud-agent-s-sift-api-token-expired-m-050e2409f0a6e500**: Fixes fraud agents retrying Sift calls with an expired credential: detects expiry style auth errors, refreshes the credential once from the secret store, and fails the case loudly if refresh does not restore access.
